# W2-03 code-anchored build spec

Decision `D-2026-10-17-w2-02-buildspec`. Implements `tasks/W2-03-cold-assessment.md` on the registered W2-02 replay. Use only accepted v4 public evidence, effective configuration and clock/source attestation. The six W2-02 manifest cases are the required phase-1 input; do not hand-insert accepted rows.

## Inputs, model and selection

Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold.py` with frozen `ColdEvidence` (`evidence_id`, `envelope_sha256`, `family`, `record_kind`, `role`, `asset_id`, `source_time`, `received_at`, `decided_at`, `quality`, `value`, `unit`, `event_id`, `correlation_id`, `outcome`, `source_id`, `clock_attested`), `ColdWindow` (`from_at`,`to_at`,`known_at`), `ColdExcursion`, `ColdStartChain` and `ColdAssessment`. Add pure `assess_excursion` and `assess_start_response`; IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:query_evidence,_configuration,_persist,enqueue` is the pattern: select `accepted_evidence` joined to `ingestion_batch`, time-effective `foundation_revision` and `binding_set_revision` via `basis_from_snapshot`, and S4-05 `batch_publication_link/evidence_publication` for provenance. Require `site_id`, `source_time >= from`, `< to`, `received_at <= known_at`, and ingestion `decided_at <= known_at`. Order `(source_time,received_at,id)`. Only exact `cold-roles-1` effective row/asset/role, GOOD, calibrated primary chamber probe and attested clock is qualifying; SUSPECT, unbound, clock-skewed and contradictory records remain linked as CONTEXT/EXCLUDED with reason. Do not choose a latest publication by display name. Select the effective Foundation and binding at each evidence source time, and refuse if multiple periods overlap. `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark at request start; never a simulated clock. Basis SHA is SHA-256 of canonical JSON with keys `analytic_id,model_version,tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,source_gate,records`; records sorted by `(evidence_id,envelope_sha256)` and include both fields. `source_gate` is the exact S4-05 classification plus source IDs, not a boolean.

`assessment_worker.py:DispatchAssessmentWorker` is the orchestration pattern. Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` with `assess_explicit` and `run_once`, using `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `cold_episode`, `cold_episode_revision`, `cold_episode_evidence`; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts/reads `finding` and `finding_revision`. Refactor both adapters to accept one caller-owned SQLAlchemy `Connection`; worker starts one transaction, persists assessment, episode, Finding and completes the job in it. No partial projection on failure. Existing dispatch methods keep their old transactions and bytes. Duplicate basis returns the existing revision; changed basis appends a revision. Relevant cold `accepted_evidence` after intake enqueues a bracketed cold job; duplicate or rejected ordinal does not. A queued/failed newer job makes previous selected read `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until the shared transaction commits.

## Rules and exact outcomes

Expected points are the 13 five-minute instants 19:00 through 20:00 in `[19:00,20:05)`; coverage numerator is distinct qualifying probe timestamps, denominator 13 (general windows derive their own aligned count), capped at 1. Need `>=0.8`, with no inferred path across a >10-minute gap. Above upper or below lower policy needs two consecutive GOOD samples at least five minutes apart; return needs two consecutive values inside band by at least effective `cold-return-hysteresis`. Start bounds `[last in-band,first out-of-band]`, end bounds `[last out-of-band,first stable in-band]`; initially out of band means unknown earliest start. Open episode has `duration_bounds.high=null`. Low duration is max(0, last continuously observed out-of-band point - latest start) for closed and open episodes, stopping at the first gap over ten minutes; high is latest return - earliest start only when both known. `degree_minute_bounds` is the sum of adjacent observed rectangles over intervals <=10 minutes using min/max exceedance; no gap area. `peak_observed` is the maximum observed HIGH sample or minimum observed LOW sample, never an interpolated extremum. Exposure Finding requires 15-minute low duration, >=0.8 coverage and source gate; otherwise POSSIBLE/UNKNOWN. No spoilage, part-failure or continuous curve claim. Normal -> `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`. Excursion -> first high at 19:15, open, duration low 45 min, high null. Backup-failed -> same exposure, door CONTEXT. Missing-probe -> 7/13, temperature UNKNOWN and no exposure Finding. Late-reading -> 12/13 at 20:05 and 13/13 at 20:10 with new basis/revision, old history retained.

Start chain groups by `(correlation_id,generator_asset_id,outage episode)` and accepted event IDs. Required steps: POWER_LOSS, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, generator state, TRANSFER_RESULT if sensor bound, bus state, compressor state, probe response. Within 30-second attested source-clock ordering tolerance, request 19:05 -> success 19:07 -> transfer 19:08 for normal. Explicit failed result 19:05 after 19:00 request supports `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with no conflicting GOOD success. Timeout from Foundation = 5 min; inferred failure from timeout additionally requires complete generator state coverage through timeout. Missing transfer is `UNKNOWN_STEP` for transfer only; bus state cannot replace it. Contradictory GOOD success and failure for same correlation -> `CONTRADICTORY`, never failure. Equal event-ID conflict is a rejected S2 ordinal, not an accepted contradictory outcome. Excluded clock skew/SUSPECT/unbound records never support claims. Claim levels are `OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY`.

Episode key is SHA-256 canonical JSON array `[tenant_id,site_id,kind,subject_asset_id,first_qualifying_event_or_crossing_evidence_id]` (UUIDs lowercase text), stored as `episode_key_sha256`. Stable anchor survives late evidence changing bounds. A newly supported different anchor after old invalidation creates a new episode and `split_from_episode_id`; old episode gets an immutable `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` revision and FindingRevision. Other transitions are `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked evidence changes and `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration/source gate/known cutoff changes. Excursion and start response have separate Findings, can share public events, and later use one overlap group. Reuse `postgres_finding_store.py:_insert_finding,_insert_revision` with domain `cold_chain`; avoid dispatch-specific candidate-minute fields.

## Migration `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py`

After `0021`, create `cold_episode(id uuid PK,tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,site_id uuid NOT NULL,kind text CHECK IN ('EXCURSION','START_RESPONSE'),asset_id uuid NOT NULL,anchor_time timestamptz NOT NULL,episode_key_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,split_from_episode_id uuid NULL,finding_id uuid NULL,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`. Unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)`; scoped composite FKs to Site, asset, optional split episode and Finding. `finding_id` stays null in this append-only slice: Finding links to episode through its immutable `claim.episode_id` and exact episode revision; no post-insert episode UPDATE fills the optional reverse pointer. Index `(site_id,kind,anchor_time)`. Create `cold_episode_revision(id uuid PK,tenant_id,site_id,episode_id uuid NOT NULL,revision_no int CHECK >0,assessment_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,basis_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,claim_level text CHECK IN ('OBSERVED','BOUNDED','POSSIBLE','UNKNOWN','CONTRADICTORY'),lower_start,upper_start,lower_end,upper_end timestamptz NULL,duration_low,duration_high,degree_minutes_low,degree_minutes_high,peak_observed numeric NULL,coverage numeric(5,4) CHECK BETWEEN 0 AND 1,missing_steps jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`. Unique `(episode_id,revision_no)` and `(episode_id,basis_sha256)`; scoped FKs to episode and assessment revision. Check each earliest/latest pair ordered when both present, low/high numeric pairs nonnegative and ordered when both present, JSON array shape, and peak null if no usable point. Index `(site_id,episode_id,revision_no DESC)`. Create `cold_episode_evidence(episode_revision_id uuid,tenant_id uuid,site_id uuid,evidence_id uuid,use text CHECK IN ('SUPPORT','CONTEXT','EXCLUDED'),reason text NULL,PRIMARY KEY(episode_revision_id,evidence_id,use))` with scoped FKs to episode revision and accepted evidence. Install append-only UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers on all three; no upsert update.

Widen `finding` and `finding_revision` from `0009_finding_store.py`: `finding.subject_asset_id uuid NULL` scoped asset FK; existing `generator_asset_id` is **text**, so keep its type and only drop NOT NULL. Add domain CHECK `mini_grid` requires generator nonnull and subject null; `cold_chain` requires subject nonnull and generator null. Add unique `(tenant_id,site_id,id,domain)`; `finding_revision.domain text NOT NULL` backfill old rows `mini_grid`, remove temporary default, replace parent FK with `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id,domain)` and preserve old mini-grid checks. For `cold_chain`, `episode_start/end`, `candidate_minutes`, `necessary_minutes`, `indeterminate_minutes` nullable; all three minute fields null. Replace bound CHECK with null-or-ordered and a separate mini-grid both-nonnull CHECK. Keep existing transition CHECK, provenance and source columns. `recompute_job.domain` is text and needs no SQL widening. Downgrade refuses if any cold episode/revision/link or cold Finding exists; otherwise restore old constraints/columns and drop only new tables/triggers/indexes. Do not delete data to force downgrade.

## Architect answers Q1-Q11 (`D-2026-10-18-w2-03-answers`)

1. **Q1, fuel trigger:** amend `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py` to drop and recreate `financial_consequence_dirty_on_finding_revision` with `AFTER INSERT ON finding_revision FOR EACH ROW WHEN (NEW.domain = 'mini_grid')`. Keep the existing `0012` function and mini-grid work behavior unchanged. Do this after adding/backfilling `finding_revision.domain` and before any cold FindingRevision can be inserted. `0022` downgrade first refuses live cold rows, drops the guarded trigger, restores the original unconditional trigger before dropping the `domain` column, and leaves the `0012` function intact. Prove a cold revision with nullable episode bounds creates zero `financial_consequence_work` rows, while a mini-grid revision still creates exactly its normal work row. W2-04 `0023` owns its separate cold consequence triggers/work; do not add cold money work to `0022`.
2. **Q2, schema:** accept the built `0022` constraints: nonnull coverage, `peak_observed IS NULL OR coverage > 0`, scoped `(tenant_id,site_id,id)` uniques needed by composite FKs, no self-split, lowercase 64-hex SHA checks. Retain the existing mini-grid nonnull bound/minute checks.
3. **Q3, identity:** store `analytic_id='cold-excursion'`, `model_version='1'` (and `cold-start-response`/`1`) in existing analytic tables. The display/model identifier `cold-excursion/1` is their joined form, not the stored analytic ID.
4. **Q4, duration:** for an open episode, `duration_low=max(0,last continuously observed out-of-band source_time - latest_start)`. A >10-minute gap stops that continuous run. The half-open window end is a selection boundary, not an observed reading. For the 19:15 to 20:00 canonical run the lower bound is **45 min**, not 50. `duration_high` and both end bounds remain null until a supported return. For a closed episode, lower duration uses the last continuously observed out-of-band point, and upper duration needs both known start and return bounds.
5. **Q5, earliest start:** null unless the preceding qualifying in-band point is no more than 10 minutes before the first out-of-band point. A preceding out-of-band or excluded point cannot supply it.
6. **Q6, levels:** coverage below `0.8` gives assessment/episode `UNKNOWN` and no exposure Finding; conflicting GOOD same-instant probe values give `CONTRADICTORY`. With adequate coverage, a confirmed excursion with lower duration at least 15 min and a permitting gate is `BOUNDED` with an exposure Finding; a shorter confirmed excursion or a closed gate is `POSSIBLE` without one. A lone out-of-band point is assessment state `UNKNOWN`, level `POSSIBLE`. Fully in-band is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED` with no excursion episode or Finding. Do not turn a closed gate into a positive Finding.
7. **Q7, start Finding:** only `OBSERVED_FAILURE` plus permitting gate opens a start-response Finding. An explicit GOOD failure without conflicting GOOD success qualifies; a timeout qualifies only with complete generator-state coverage through its effective deadline. `OBSERVED_SUCCESS`, `UNKNOWN` and `CONTRADICTORY` do not open one.
8. **Q8, transfer:** only a qualifying `TRANSFER_RESULT` before the first `POWER_RESTORED` in the same correlation counts as backup transfer. Bus state can be observed while `TRANSFER` remains `MISSING`; a post-restoration grid transfer cannot close the backup step.
9. **Q9, peak:** for HIGH, `peak_observed=max(qualifying observed temperatures)`; for LOW, `min(...)`. Preserve the direction with the episode result/read. No interpolation or mock value supplies the peak.
10. **Q10, calibration:** resolve calibration while selecting the effective Foundation/`cold-roles-1` binding and primary room probe via `probe_asset_id`; pass only that eligible probe to the pure `ColdEvidence` rule. The pure record has no calibration field. Missing, duplicate or overlapping effective calibration/role periods fail closed.
11. **Q11, contradiction:** link each mutually conflicting accepted GOOD record as `cold_episode_evidence.use='EXCLUDED', reason='CONTRADICTORY'` (and show it in read exclusions). An S2 `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` ordinal is not accepted evidence and never becomes this analytic contradiction.

## Commit-3 confirmations and commit-4 freshness amendment (2026-10-18)

Accept the 5-minute-aligned bracket of accepted cold source times as a work window, coalescing pending cold jobs and merging assessed windows within the rule's 10-minute continuity bridge. The six-case replay selects exactly `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`; a gap over 10 minutes does not bridge, and unrelated Site/domain work does not merge.

A still-supported episode whose evidence or basis no longer warrants its Finding receives `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`, respectively, and a new FindingRevision with lifecycle `UNCONFIRMED`. Keep the episode and prior revisions. Reserve `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` for invalidated episode support and the split path.

Accept the remaining commit-3 interpretations: (2) replay tests record a labelled simulated SYSTEM clock attestation per Lab source before authenticated upload, with no direct accepted-row insert; (3) a test-only registered-source `classify` double proves the positive `split_from_finding_id` link, while an ordinary ad hoc gateway reading stays MIXED and opens no new Finding; (5) W2-00's no-dispatch-job assertion ignores `cold_chain` jobs, which newly accepted relevant v4 cold rows may enqueue; (6) the excursion replay's incidental start-failure Finding is not an asserted excursion outcome because that replay declares no expected start result, while the backup-failed start assertion remains required.

**Freshness reference for commit 4:** This supersedes the â€œat the read's timeâ€ sentence below. Evaluate the 10-minute probe recency at the selected half-open window's `to` boundary, using the latest qualifying probe `source_time` in that window. This is window-scoped freshness, not live gateway contact. Thus a 20:00 probe in the 2026-10-06 replay window ending 20:05 can be `CURRENT` for that window when opened later. Show the actual window, evidence `as_of`, committed disposition watermark and registered-simulation provenance; never call that replay live or move its timestamps. State precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit `known_at` older than the latest committed relevant disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection, no qualifying probe, or a future `to` boundary -> `UNKNOWN`; latest qualifying probe more than 10 minutes before `to` -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. An explicit historical cutoff stays historical even if probe recency passes. `known_at` remains the committed disposition cutoff and is not the freshness clock. Test later wall-clock replay viewing, an actually stale selected window, an older explicit cutoff and a pending newer job.

## Commits 3-5 exact contract

**Commit 3 â€” transaction and projection.** Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` in `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py` with `assess_explicit(tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at)` and `run_once(tenant_id=None,site_id=None)`; use `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'` and bracket relevant accepted cold source times into work windows. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone queries typed v4 accepted rows and inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `analytic_run_assessment`, `assessment_revision`, `analytic_run_selection`, and the three `cold_episode*` tables. `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts cold `finding`, `finding_revision`, `finding_revision_assessment` and `finding_revision_evidence`. Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.

Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.

The canonical selected window is `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`: 13 scheduled probe instants, 19:00 through 20:00. Expected accepted-replay results: normal 13/13, `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`/`BOUNDED`, observed successful start and transfer, zero exposure Findings; excursion 13/13, first HIGH at 19:15, start bounds 19:10â€“19:15, open end, duration `{low:'45',high:null}`, peak `'4.8'`, one bounded exposure Finding; backup-failed 13/13, `OBSERVED_FAILURE`/`OBSERVED` start Finding, `TRANSFER` missing, door CONTEXT, plus one exposure Finding; missing-probe 7/13, `UNKNOWN` temperature/no exposure Finding, start failure still observed, gap 19:15â€“19:45; late-reading at 20:05 cutoff 12/13 with 19:20 absent, at 20:10 cutoff 13/13 and a new immutable basis/revision with the same first-HIGH anchor; contradictory GOOD start outcomes `CONTRADICTORY`, no observed-failure Finding. The pinned pure vectors and replay dispositions, not v69 illustration values, set other decimals and evidence UUIDs.

**Commit 4 â€” selected reads.** Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold_reads.py:ColdReadService` and `backend/assetops_backend/cold_assessment_api.py:build_cold_assessment_router`. The four GET routes are `/api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature`, `/backup-chain`, `/findings`, and `/findings/{id}`. Accept strict UTC `from,to,known_at`; enforce `from<to`, half-open maximum 31 days, tenant and COLDCHAIN Site scope; default `known_at` to the committed disposition watermark captured at request start. Use a single consistent selected snapshot for all objects and S4-05 source gate. Common envelope: `site_id`, `window:{from,to,known_at}`, `currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since}`, `source_provenance:{classification,source_ids,evidence_ids,basis_sha256}`, `limitations:string[]`. Every object/card includes `as_of:UTC|null` and `limitation:string|null`; decimal scalars are strings or null. `watermark` identifies the included disposition time and selected assessment revision; never use the simulator clock. States are exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`. A pending newer job returns the previous selected revision only as pending, without a current claim; an explicit unresolved revision/basis request is 409. Historical cutoff remains historical after newer evidence. Current state requires the latest relevant probe no older than 10 minutes at the read's time. Valid absent assessment/claim is 200 `UNKNOWN` with null values and empty arrays; infrastructure failure is 503, never an empty success. Refusals use `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 auth, 403 wrong tenant or disallowed source disclosure, 404 missing/wrong-type Site or unknown scoped Finding, 409 unsettled selection/cursor basis, 422 malformed UTC/range/unknown parameter, 503 unavailable store.

`temperature` adds `policy:{low_deg_c,high_deg_c,return_hysteresis_deg_c,min_excursion_min,probe_asset_id,room_asset_id,as_of,limitation}`, `points:[{at,value,quality,evidence_id,as_of,limitation}]`, `coverage:{known_points,expected_points,ratio}`, `gaps:[{start,end,reason,expected_points,received_points,as_of,limitation}]`, `excursions:[{episode_id,revision_id,direction,start_bounds:{earliest,latest},end_bounds:{earliest,latest},duration_bounds:{low,high},degree_minute_bounds:{low,high},peak_observed,claim_level,as_of,limitation}]`, `excluded:[{evidence_id,reason,as_of,limitation}]`. `backup-chain` adds `chains:[{correlation_id,episode_id,revision_id,steps:[{kind,at,outcome,evidence_id,state,as_of,limitation}],missing_steps,claim_level,start_timeout_min,as_of,limitation}]`, `contradictions:[{correlation_id,evidence_ids,reason,as_of,limitation}]`; step order is GRID, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, GENERATOR, TRANSFER when bound, BUS, COMPRESSOR, PROBE. Missing step has null time/outcome/evidence ID. `findings` is `{items:[{finding_id,kind,subject_asset_id,revision_id,lifecycle,claim_level,title,episode_bounds,limitations,assessment_revision_id,evidence_ids,links:{finding,evidence,analysis},as_of,limitation}],next_cursor}`; detail has those selected fields plus `basis_sha256`, `episode_id`, `episode_revision_id`, `revisions:[{revision_id,revision_no,transition,lifecycle,claim_level,basis_sha256,created_at,evidence_ids,as_of,limitation}]`. `episode_bounds` has `{start:{earliest,latest},end:{earliest,latest}}`. Each evidence/Analysis link retains the selected window/cutoff and exact accepted/revision ID. List ordering is `(anchor_time,finding_id)`, cursor is base64url canonical `(time,id)` bound to the selected basis, default limit 50/max 200; never page across a changed basis. Filter every Finding query by `domain='cold_chain'`, tenant, Site and selected window; existing mini-grid Finding reads remain unchanged.

## Known deviations and interpretations

1. Work window: job window = 5-min-aligned bracket of its accepted source times merged with selected cold windows within the 10-min bridge; ledger coalesces pending cold jobs across the same bridge (canonical replay yields exactly `[19:00,20:05)`). Architect confirmation requested.
2. Clock attestation: replay tests record the SYSTEM attester's labelled simulated attestation per lab source (epoch `cold-<case>`) before upload; not an accepted-row insert.
3. L3 `split_from_finding_id`: an ad hoc reading enters only outside the registered publication, so the gate is MIXED and no new Finding opens (asserted); the positive link is proven with a test-only `classify` double.
4. A Finding whose episode no longer warrants one (still supported, claim limited) gets `SUPPORT_CHANGED`/`BASIS_REFRESHED` with lifecycle `UNCONFIRMED` (spec silent).
5. `test_w200_source_events_postgres.py` "no dispatch job" now excludes `cold_chain`.
6. W2-02 excursion replay's start chain is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` (opens a start Finding too); not asserted (spec silent).
7. Placement: SQL `ColdReadService` sits read-only at the end of `postgres_assessment_store.py` (rule-8 driver allowlist); pure rules stay in `cold_reads.py`. Guard not loosened.
8. Default window: neither `from` nor `to` -> latest selected cold window at the cutoff; only one -> 422.
9. Selection: latest `analytic_run_selection.seq` per analytic covering the request with run cutoff <= `known_at`; points filtered to the request; freshness at the selected window's `to`.
10. Pending: PENDING/LEASED/FAILED cold job overlapping the window widened by the 10-min bridge.
11. 403 source disclosure: injectable `disclosure(code, tenant, site)` seam, default allow (no production policy yet); MIXED class is shown in provenance, not 403. The UI's 403 page refusal is test-only for that reason.
12. 409 only for the cursor-basis case (a selection always joins a settled revision).
13. Findings envelope `basis_sha256` = sha256_json of the two analytic bases; detail uses its revision basis; `as_of` rules as in the handoff.
14. Links: evidence `href` keys on `message_id`; Analysis `/api/sites/{code}/assessments/{id}`. The UI links exact accepted IDs to the existing Evidence Analysis record route (`?view=analysis&record=`) and the assessment revision to `?view=analysis&assessment=`; whether that existing view renders a cold record/revision was not verified (residual risk).
15. Missing-probe replay: excursion episode (claim UNKNOWN) without an exposure Finding (asserted).
16. Unbuilt in-page entries: the mock's Cooling subtab and the cold Finding Recommendation tab are not rendered. The navigation guard fixes the unbuilt-entry inventory (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`) and "a new unbuilt entry is a ruling"; first attempt failed the guard and was withdrawn. Ruling requested.
17. Mock row "Loading / batch" renders as "Stock loading / batch": the capture tool treats any "Loading" text as a page still loading (W2-01 deviation 6 precedent).
18. Vocabulary: the v69 title "Claim boundary", the mock columns "Power to load"/"Load response" and the spec-required "Registered simulation" provenance are on the W1 list (`claim`, `load`, `simulation`). The spec binds titles to the JSON and columns to the mock, so they are kept and filtered by exact text in the test's scan only; an approved title change is the Architect's (as for `asset:3`). Ruling requested.
19. Refused cold Finding (404 etc.): the kind is unknown, so the page renders the page refusal plus the `cold-finding` slots with gaps.
20. "Manual recovery" card: no operational-log source exists, so it reads "Not recorded"; no Incident link is drawn (no Incident record).
21. Chart y-range is the data/policy range plus 1 Â°C; the mock's illustration axis (0-10 Â°C) is not copied.

.agent\architect-W2-03-q2-log.txt:1217:80-**W2-01 contact null closure (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`).** `contact.state` is never null in a successful cold Foundation/readiness response. If there is no gateway-health store or no matching selected gateway/diagnostic, return `NEVER` with `as_of:null`; do not manufacture a receive time. For a registered selected gateway with no diagnostic, the UI may say `No contact received yet`. If no gateway is selected or registered, say `No gateway selected`/`No gateway registered` and do not say that a particular gateway was never contacted. Preserve a typed limitation identifying the absent health read or gateway match. A health-read error is a read failure with an explicit unavailable limitation, not evidence for a recent or stale contact state. `NEVER` here means no contact is recorded in the available selected read; it proves neither equipment condition nor physical non-contact. Cover both missing-store and unmatched-gateway cases, including API enum, null `as_of`, and UI wording, in W2-01 review tests.

.agent\architect-W2-04-gate-log.txt:7025:backend/assetops_backend/sites/cold_foundation.py:70:# D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact: why a successful read has no contact time. Each maps to NEVER with as_of null
.agent\architect-W2-04-gate-log.txt:7037:.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:80:**W2-01 contact null closure (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`).** `contact.state` is never null in a successful cold Foundation/readiness response. If there is no gateway-health store or no matching selected gateway/diagnostic, return `NEVER` with `as_of:null`; do not manufacture a receive time. For a registered selected gateway with no diagnostic, the UI may say `No contact received yet`. If no gateway is selected or registered, say `No gateway selected`/`No gateway registered` and do not say that a particular gateway was never contacted. Preserve a typed limitation identifying the absent health read or gateway match. A health-read error is a read failure with an explicit unavailable limitation, not evidence for a recent or stale contact state. `NEVER` here means no contact is recorded in the available selected read; it proves neither equipment condition nor physical non-contact. Cover both missing-store and unmatched-gateway cases, including API enum, null `as_of`, and UI wording, in W2-01 review tests.

.agent\planner-W2-04-log.txt:325:**W2-01 contact null closure (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`).** `contact.state` is never null in a successful cold Foundation/readiness response. If there is no gateway-health store or no matching selected gateway/diagnostic, return `NEVER` with `as_of:null`; do not manufacture a receive time. For a registered selected gateway with no diagnostic, the UI may say `No contact received yet`. If no gateway is selected or registered, say `No gateway selected`/`No gateway registered` and do not say that a particular gateway was never contacted. Preserve a typed limitation identifying the absent health read or gateway match. A health-read error is a read failure with an explicit unavailable limitation, not evidence for a recent or stale contact state. `NEVER` here means no contact is recorded in the available selected read; it proves neither equipment condition nor physical non-contact. Cover both missing-store and unmatched-gateway cases, including API enum, null `as_of`, and UI wording, in W2-01 review tests.
.agent\planner-W2-04-log.txt:417:| `temperature` | **3 Claim boundary** â†’ `temperature.excursions[].claim_level,excluded,coverage,gaps` | No spoilage or precise interpolation claim; limitation remains beside the chart. |
.agent\planner-W2-04-log.txt:717:.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:80:**W2-01 contact null closure (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`).** `contact.state` is never null in a successful cold Foundation/readiness response. If there is no gateway-health store or no matching selected gateway/diagnostic, return `NEVER` with `as_of:null`; do not manufacture a receive time. For a registered selected gateway with no diagnostic, the UI may say `No contact received yet`. If no gateway is selected or registered, say `No gateway selected`/`No gateway registered` and do not say that a particular gateway was never contacted. Preserve a typed limitation identifying the absent health read or gateway match. A health-read error is a read failure with an explicit unavailable limitation, not evidence for a recent or stale contact state. `NEVER` here means no contact is recorded in the available selected read; it proves neither equipment condition nor physical non-contact. Cover both missing-store and unmatched-gateway cases, including API enum, null `as_of`, and UI wording, in W2-01 review tests.
.agent\planner-W2-04-log.txt:815:Decision: `D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`. Codex Architect extends the owner's full-v69-menu choice to the named W1 in-page rows as a narrow exception to the absent-Site-tab rule: show live registered destinations and visibly/accessibly `Not available yet` noninteractive entries for unbuilt Foundation, Work, Finding and Financials destinations. Site Findings uses the mock lens chips; only lenses backed by a selected Site/window classification and filter become interactive, while the date/window scope control remains. Exact F5 inventory, presentation and guard proof are in `.agent/architect-W1-fidelity.md` (F5 section). W2-01 cold contact maps missing health store or unmatched gateway/diagnostic to `NEVER`, `as_of:null`, with distinct no-gateway/no-contact wording and an honest limitation; a true read failure remains explicitly unavailable. Exact contact contract is in `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` (W2-01 contact null closure). .agent\W2-technical-design-cold.md:83:**W2-01 contact null closure (`D-2026-10-17-f5-subtabs-and-contact`).** `contact.state` is never null in a successful cold Foundation/readiness response. If there is no gateway-health store or no matching selected gateway/diagnostic, return `NEVER` with `as_of:null`; do not manufacture a receive time. For a registered selected gateway with no diagnostic, the UI may say `No contact received yet`. If no gateway is selected or registered, say `No gateway selected`/`No gateway registered` and do not say that a particular gateway was never contacted. Preserve a typed limitation identifying the absent health read or gateway match. A health-read error is a read failure with an explicit unavailable limitation, not evidence for a recent or stale contact state. `NEVER` here means no contact is recorded in the available selected read; it proves neither equipment condition nor physical non-contact. Cover both missing-store and unmatched-gateway cases, including API enum, null `as_of`, and UI wording, in W2-01 review tests.

.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md-136-Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md-137-
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md-138-Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never “no excursion” from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md-139-
.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md:140:Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST → START_REQUEST → START_RESULT → generator RUNNING/FAILED → optional TRANSFER_RESULT → bus POWERED/UNPOWERED → compressor state → probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

## Commit-3 confirmations and commit-4 freshness amendment (2026-10-18)

Accept the 5-minute-aligned bracket of accepted cold source times as a work window, coalescing pending cold jobs and merging assessed windows within the rule's 10-minute continuity bridge. The six-case replay selects exactly `[2026-10-06T19:00:00Z,2026-10-06T20:05:00Z)`; a gap over 10 minutes does not bridge, and unrelated Site/domain work does not merge.

A still-supported episode whose evidence or basis no longer warrants its Finding receives `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`, respectively, and a new FindingRevision with lifecycle `UNCONFIRMED`. Keep the episode and prior revisions. Reserve `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` for invalidated episode support and the split path.

Accept the remaining commit-3 interpretations: (2) replay tests record a labelled simulated SYSTEM clock attestation per Lab source before authenticated upload, with no direct accepted-row insert; (3) a test-only registered-source `classify` double proves the positive `split_from_finding_id` link, while an ordinary ad hoc gateway reading stays MIXED and opens no new Finding; (5) W2-00's no-dispatch-job assertion ignores `cold_chain` jobs, which newly accepted relevant v4 cold rows may enqueue; (6) the excursion replay's incidental start-failure Finding is not an asserted excursion outcome because that replay declares no expected start result, while the backup-failed start assertion remains required.

**Freshness reference for commit 4:** This supersedes the â€œat the read's timeâ€ sentence below. Evaluate the 10-minute probe recency at the selected half-open window's `to` boundary, using the latest qualifying probe `source_time` in that window. This is window-scoped freshness, not live gateway contact. Thus a 20:00 probe in the 2026-10-06 replay window ending 20:05 can be `CURRENT` for that window when opened later. Show the actual window, evidence `as_of`, committed disposition watermark and registered-simulation provenance; never call that replay live or move its timestamps. State precedence: overlapping queued/failed newer work -> `PENDING_RECOMPUTE`; explicit `known_at` older than the latest committed relevant disposition -> `HISTORICAL`; no settled selection, no qualifying probe, or a future `to` boundary -> `UNKNOWN`; latest qualifying probe more than 10 minutes before `to` -> `STALE_SOURCE`; otherwise `CURRENT`. An explicit historical cutoff stays historical even if probe recency passes. `known_at` remains the committed disposition cutoff and is not the freshness clock. Test later wall-clock replay viewing, an actually stale selected window, an older explicit cutoff and a pending newer job.

## Commits 3-5 exact contract

**Commit 3 â€” transaction and projection.** Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` in `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/assessment_worker.py` with `assess_explicit(tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at)` and `run_once(tenant_id=None,site_id=None)`; use `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'` and bracket relevant accepted cold source times into work windows. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone queries typed v4 accepted rows and inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `analytic_run_assessment`, `assessment_revision`, `analytic_run_selection`, and the three `cold_episode*` tables. `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts cold `finding`, `finding_revision`, `finding_revision_assessment` and `finding_revision_evidence`. Add caller-connection methods to the two stores while preserving existing dispatch methods and their transaction behavior. The worker opens one SQLAlchemy transaction, rechecks the lease/site/input fence, selects effective configuration and S4-05 lineage, computes both analytics, persists their exact revisions, episodes and Findings, and completes the job before commit. Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.

Use `analytic_id='cold-excursion'|'cold-start-response'`, `model_version='1'`, window `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at`, and the exact canonical basis described above. In `assessment_revision.result`, persist the pure `ColdAssessment` fields and its episode/chain data with decimal strings, UTC instants and exact accepted IDs; use a stable assessment key per analytic/Site/window, and append when the basis changes. `cold_episode.kind` is `EXCURSION|START_RESPONSE`; excursion `asset_id` is the effective room, chain `asset_id` the generator. Its SHA key is the canonical five-part array already specified; anchor on first qualifying crossing/request evidence ID, never a bound time that a late record can move. A confirmed but claim-limited excursion can have an episode without a Finding; an observed successful start can have a chain episode without a Finding; no excursion episode is invented for an in-band or single unconfirmed point. Map `start_earliest/start_latest/end_earliest/end_latest` to `lower_start/upper_start/lower_end/upper_end`, duration and degree-minute bounds to the corresponding numeric columns, coverage to a four-decimal ratio, and start-chain `missing_steps` to its JSON array. Start-response duration/degree-minute/peak columns are null; its coverage is `0` because those are temperature metrics. Episode evidence links cover the exact SUPPORT, CONTEXT and EXCLUDED records in the pure result, with no private world IDs. Identical selected basis returns existing revisions; changed basis appends. A returned old basis after an intervening revision must append a new FindingRevision even though its SHA appeared earlier. On anchor invalidation append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` to the old episode/Finding, create the new episode with `split_from_episode_id`, and set the new Finding's `split_from_finding_id` to the old Finding when there was one. Use `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked accepted evidence changes; use `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration, source gate or cutoff changes. Store cold Finding `domain='cold_chain'`, `subject_asset_id`, null `generator_asset_id` and null dispatch minute fields; `claim` contains `episode_id` and exact `episode_revision_id`. Never call the dispatch-shaped `_insert_finding/_insert_revision` unchanged: add domain-specific cold insert paths under the same owner. Prove all six `test_w203_cold_replay_postgres.py` cases through authenticated Batch V2 intake, plus no mini-grid fuel work from cold FindingRevision.

.ai/DECISIONS.md:4063:**Dispatch rule.** The first Postgres model is a **new version** of the mini-grid dispatch rule; it may reuse the published `dispatch-2` thresholds/decision table but must name every carried or changed rule in a versioned table and compare its results. Reconstruct generator ON/OFF intervals with observed bounds/gaps. For each segment, apply evidenced constraints in order: an authorized, supported override or effective minimum runtime; battery reserve/near-reserve; PV-plus-battery capability shortfall; then candidate headroom. A whole interval is Necessary only if every assessed segment is Necessary; any supported Candidate segment gives `CANDIDATE_AVOIDABLE` only for those bounded segments; otherwise it is Indeterminate. Missing role, poor quality, contradiction, open bound or ambiguity names the affected segment/limit. Candidate requires accepted **available PV power** and PCS **discharge capability** (the published `dispatch-2` PV-alone branch also requires evidenced PCS availability), plus the load and, for battery-supported headroom, SOC and selected Foundation reserve basis; observed `pv_power` and rated/actual `battery_power` are not substitutes. The optional available-power and discharge-capability roles require a separately published, versioned binding vocabulary and actual registered readings before S3 can make a candidate claim; `minigrid-roles-1` remains historically interpretable. The S1 Foundation's time-valid `battery_reserve` control is authoritative; S2's measurement-only V3 path supplies no accepted POLICY/OPERATIONAL_RECORD override. Do not fabricate one from a Foundation change or Lab event. Missing optional override records do not by themselves block a Candidate, but the result must say no command record was observed, not that no command occurred; any conflicting supported constraint makes the affected portion Indeterminate under the versioned rule. A supported policy/command record later needs its own versioned provenance and conflict contract before it can change this rule. Necessary policy-only claims require that supported record; otherwise classify from evidenced physical constraints or Indeterminate. Dispatch states a technical candidate, not recovered fuel/cost, physical equipment condition, causal intent or a Finding; S4 owns Finding identity and money.

# W2-03 code-anchored build spec

Decision `D-2026-10-17-w2-02-buildspec`. Implements `tasks/W2-03-cold-assessment.md` on the registered W2-02 replay. Use only accepted v4 public evidence, effective configuration and clock/source attestation. The six W2-02 manifest cases are the required phase-1 input; do not hand-insert accepted rows.

## Inputs, model and selection

Add `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/cold.py` with frozen `ColdEvidence` (`evidence_id`, `envelope_sha256`, `family`, `record_kind`, `role`, `asset_id`, `source_time`, `received_at`, `decided_at`, `quality`, `value`, `unit`, `event_id`, `correlation_id`, `outcome`, `source_id`, `clock_attested`), `ColdWindow` (`from_at`,`to_at`,`known_at`), `ColdExcursion`, `ColdStartChain` and `ColdAssessment`. Add pure `assess_excursion` and `assess_start_response`; IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. `backend/assetops_backend/analytics/adapters/postgres_assessment_store.py:query_evidence,_configuration,_persist,enqueue` is the pattern: select `accepted_evidence` joined to `ingestion_batch`, time-effective `foundation_revision` and `binding_set_revision` via `basis_from_snapshot`, and S4-05 `batch_publication_link/evidence_publication` for provenance. Require `site_id`, `source_time >= from`, `< to`, `received_at <= known_at`, and ingestion `decided_at <= known_at`. Order `(source_time,received_at,id)`. Only exact `cold-roles-1` effective row/asset/role, GOOD, calibrated primary chamber probe and attested clock is qualifying; SUSPECT, unbound, clock-skewed and contradictory records remain linked as CONTEXT/EXCLUDED with reason. Do not choose a latest publication by display name. Select the effective Foundation and binding at each evidence source time, and refuse if multiple periods overlap. `known_at` defaults to latest committed disposition watermark at request start; never a simulated clock. Basis SHA is SHA-256 of canonical JSON with keys `analytic_id,model_version,tenant_id,site_id,window,known_at,foundation_revision_ids,binding_revision_ids,source_gate,records`; records sorted by `(evidence_id,envelope_sha256)` and include both fields. `source_gate` is the exact S4-05 classification plus source IDs, not a boolean.

`assessment_worker.py:DispatchAssessmentWorker` is the orchestration pattern. Add `ColdAssessmentWorker` with `assess_explicit` and `run_once`, using `recompute_job.domain='cold_chain'`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `cold_episode`, `cold_episode_revision`, `cold_episode_evidence`; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts/reads `finding` and `finding_revision`. Refactor both adapters to accept one caller-owned SQLAlchemy `Connection`; worker starts one transaction, persists assessment, episode, Finding and completes the job in it. No partial projection on failure. Existing dispatch methods keep their old transactions and bytes. Duplicate basis returns the existing revision; changed basis appends a revision. Relevant cold `accepted_evidence` after intake enqueues a bracketed cold job; duplicate or rejected ordinal does not. A queued/failed newer job makes previous selected read `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until the shared transaction commits.

## Rules and exact outcomes

Expected points are the 13 five-minute instants 19:00 through 20:00 in `[19:00,20:05)`; coverage numerator is distinct qualifying probe timestamps, denominator 13 (general windows derive their own aligned count), capped at 1. Need `>=0.8`, with no inferred path across a >10-minute gap. Above upper or below lower policy needs two consecutive GOOD samples at least five minutes apart; return needs two consecutive values inside band by at least effective `cold-return-hysteresis`. Start bounds `[last in-band,first out-of-band]`, end bounds `[last out-of-band,first stable in-band]`; initially out of band means unknown earliest start. Open episode has `duration_bounds.high=null`. Low duration is max(0, last continuously observed out-of-band point - latest start) for closed and open episodes, stopping at the first gap over ten minutes; high is latest return - earliest start only when both known. `degree_minute_bounds` is the sum of adjacent observed rectangles over intervals <=10 minutes using min/max exceedance; no gap area. `peak_observed` is the maximum observed HIGH sample or minimum observed LOW sample, never an interpolated extremum. Exposure Finding requires 15-minute low duration, >=0.8 coverage and source gate; otherwise POSSIBLE/UNKNOWN. No spoilage, part-failure or continuous curve claim. Normal -> `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED`. Excursion -> first high at 19:15, open, duration low 45 min, high null. Backup-failed -> same exposure, door CONTEXT. Missing-probe -> 7/13, temperature UNKNOWN and no exposure Finding. Late-reading -> 12/13 at 20:05 and 13/13 at 20:10 with new basis/revision, old history retained.

Start chain groups by `(correlation_id,generator_asset_id,outage episode)` and accepted event IDs. Required steps: POWER_LOSS, START_REQUEST, START_RESULT, generator state, TRANSFER_RESULT if sensor bound, bus state, compressor state, probe response. Within 30-second attested source-clock ordering tolerance, request 19:05 -> success 19:07 -> transfer 19:08 for normal. Explicit failed result 19:05 after 19:00 request supports `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with no conflicting GOOD success. Timeout from Foundation = 5 min; inferred failure from timeout additionally requires complete generator state coverage through timeout. Missing transfer is `UNKNOWN_STEP` for transfer only; bus state cannot replace it. Contradictory GOOD success and failure for same correlation -> `CONTRADICTORY`, never failure. Equal event-ID conflict is a rejected S2 ordinal, not an accepted contradictory outcome. Excluded clock skew/SUSPECT/unbound records never support claims. Claim levels are `OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY`.

Episode key is SHA-256 canonical JSON array `[tenant_id,site_id,kind,subject_asset_id,first_qualifying_event_or_crossing_evidence_id]` (UUIDs lowercase text), stored as `episode_key_sha256`. Stable anchor survives late evidence changing bounds. A newly supported different anchor after old invalidation creates a new episode and `split_from_episode_id`; old episode gets an immutable `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` revision and FindingRevision. Other transitions are `SUPPORT_CHANGED` when linked evidence changes and `BASIS_REFRESHED` when only effective configuration/source gate/known cutoff changes. Excursion and start response have separate Findings, can share public events, and later use one overlap group. Reuse `postgres_finding_store.py:_insert_finding,_insert_revision` with domain `cold_chain`; avoid dispatch-specific candidate-minute fields.

## Migration `0022_w2_cold_assessment.py`

After `0021`, create `cold_episode(id uuid PK,tenant_id uuid NOT NULL,site_id uuid NOT NULL,kind text CHECK IN ('EXCURSION','START_RESPONSE'),asset_id uuid NOT NULL,anchor_time timestamptz NOT NULL,episode_key_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,split_from_episode_id uuid NULL,finding_id uuid NULL,created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`. Unique `(tenant_id,site_id,episode_key_sha256)`; scoped composite FKs to Site, asset, optional split episode and Finding. `finding_id` stays null in this append-only slice: Finding links to episode through its immutable `claim.episode_id` and exact episode revision; no post-insert episode UPDATE fills the optional reverse pointer. Index `(site_id,kind,anchor_time)`. Create `cold_episode_revision(id uuid PK,tenant_id,site_id,episode_id uuid NOT NULL,revision_no int CHECK >0,assessment_revision_id uuid NOT NULL,basis_sha256 char(64) NOT NULL,claim_level text CHECK IN ('OBSERVED','BOUNDED','POSSIBLE','UNKNOWN','CONTRADICTORY'),lower_start,upper_start,lower_end,upper_end timestamptz NULL,duration_low,duration_high,degree_minutes_low,degree_minutes_high,peak_observed numeric NULL,coverage numeric(5,4) CHECK BETWEEN 0 AND 1,missing_steps jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',created_at timestamptz NOT NULL)`. Unique `(episode_id,revision_no)` and `(episode_id,basis_sha256)`; scoped FKs to episode and assessment revision. Check each earliest/latest pair ordered when both present, low/high numeric pairs nonnegative and ordered when both present, JSON array shape, and peak null if no usable point. Index `(site_id,episode_id,revision_no DESC)`. Create `cold_episode_evidence(episode_revision_id uuid,tenant_id uuid,site_id uuid,evidence_id uuid,use text CHECK IN ('SUPPORT','CONTEXT','EXCLUDED'),reason text NULL,PRIMARY KEY(episode_revision_id,evidence_id,use))` with scoped FKs to episode revision and accepted evidence. Install append-only UPDATE/DELETE refusal triggers on all three; no upsert update.

Widen `finding` and `finding_revision` from `0009_finding_store.py`: `finding.subject_asset_id uuid NULL` scoped asset FK; existing `generator_asset_id` is **text**, so keep its type and only drop NOT NULL. Add domain CHECK `mini_grid` requires generator nonnull and subject null; `cold_chain` requires subject nonnull and generator null. Add unique `(tenant_id,site_id,id,domain)`; `finding_revision.domain text NOT NULL` backfill old rows `mini_grid`, remove temporary default, replace parent FK with `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id,domain)` and preserve old mini-grid checks. For `cold_chain`, `episode_start/end`, `candidate_minutes`, `necessary_minutes`, `indeterminate_minutes` nullable; all three minute fields null. Replace bound CHECK with null-or-ordered and a separate mini-grid both-nonnull CHECK. Keep existing transition CHECK, provenance and source columns. `recompute_job.domain` is text and needs no SQL widening. Downgrade refuses if any cold episode/revision/link or cold Finding exists; otherwise restore old constraints/columns and drop only new tables/triggers/indexes. Do not delete data to force downgrade.

Failure rolls back *all* projection rows and completion, then records a retryable job failure; a stale lease never commits. Acquire the same Site/domain advisory lock for competing cold projections. The relevant intake enqueue must occur only for a new accepted cold ordinal, including a late one; duplicate/rejected ordinals enqueue nothing. Configuration or source-gate changes must enqueue affected selected windows as well, or the read stays explicitly stale/pending until a controlled reconciliation enqueues them.
